Simpler: At each outer iteration:
1. Compute b=conv(a,a), M=max b.
2. Choose weights λ_k = softmax((b_k-M)/β) ( sums1) or uniform on near-max.
3. Solve convex subproblem min ∑λ_k b_k over simplex a≥0, sum=1 using projected FISTA with λ fixed. This gives a candidate a_new.
4. Underrelax a = (1-t)a + t a_new, t adaptive by score.
5. Repeat.

```
def _sg_polish(a, deadline, max_iter=5000, lr=0.01):
  z=log(a); best=a; best_sc=score
  step=lr
  znorm = norm(z)
  for it in range(max_iter):
    if time>deadline: break
    a=softmax(z)
    c=conv(a,a); M=max(c)
    # active weights: use soft with beta = M*1e-4 maybe plus top active?
    beta = M*1e-4
    shifted=(c-M)/beta; e=exp(clip(shifted, -50? 0)); e normalized. If beta very small, e effectively one-hot at max.
    h=conv(e,a_rev)[n-1:2n-1]; W=dot(e,c); grad = a*(h-W)
    # normalize grad maybe
    gnorm=norm(grad)
    if gnorm small break
    # generate candidate steps: step, step/2, step/4... and maybe opposite? Only descent direction is -grad (since grad of score wrt z). z_new=z - step*grad/gnorm? Use scaled step in [0.01,?].
    best_z_local=z; best_sc_local=best_sc
    for trial in [step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.1, 2*step?]:
       zn=z - trial*grad/gnorm
       zn=clip(zn,-50,50); zn-
       a_t=softmax(zn); sc=score(a_t)
       if sc<best_sc_local: update
    if improved: best=softmax; step=min(step*1.1, lr*2); else step*=0.5; if step<1e-5: add noise/perturb or break
```

`_conv(a,b)`:
```
def _conv(a,b):
    n,m=len(a),len(b)
    out=n+m-1
    N=1
    while N<out: N*=2
    N=max(2,N)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)[:out]
    # numerical small negative? For nonnegative, possible tiny negative; set max(0)? For score max, negative irrelevant; for gradient weights c may have tiny negative but active max positive. We can do r=np.maximum(r,0) to maintain nonnegative. But for smooth objective, clipping may affect gradient slightly. Use np.maximum(r,0).
    return r
```
For gradient, c should be nonnegative; clipping okay.

`_score(a)`:
```
def _score(a):
 a=np.asarray(a,float)
 a=np.clip(a,0,1e6)
 s=a.sum()
 if s<1e-12: return np.inf
 c=_conv(a,a)
 return float(2*len(a)*c.max()/(s*s))
```
Scoring with unnormalized parent? okay.

betas=[base_beta*factor for factor in [1,0.5,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03,0.015,0.007,0.003,0.001,0.0004,0.00015,0.00006,0.00002]] maybe too many.

`_make_power(n,p)`:
```
if n<=1: return np.ones(n)
t=np.arange(n)+1.0
a=t**p
s=a.sum()
if s<=0: a=np.ones(n)
return a/s
```
For p negative, t^p okay.

`_make_sqrt(n)`:
```
a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(4*i)
s=a.sum(); if s>0 a/=s
else uniform
```
This is O(n), fine.

`_make_logistic(n,s,m)`:
`x=np.linspace(0,1,n); a=1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-s*(x-m)))`; normalize. Ensure no overflow: s up to 10, x-m ±1, exp safe.

`_make_random_log(n, amp=0.5, smooth=16)`:
```
z=_RNG.standard_normal(n)
if smooth>1: k=min(smooth,n); z=np.convolve(z, np.ones(k)/k, 'same')
z*=amp
a=_softmax(z)
```

`_interpolate(a,n)`:
```
x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(a)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a); b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s<1e-12: b=np.ones(n); else b/=s
```

def _update_best(best_seq,best_sc,seq,sc,label):

  if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=_score(seq)
  if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
     print(...)

`_run_lbfgs` details:
```
def _run_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, tag=""):
  a=_normalize(a_init); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
  n=len(a); z=_to_z(a); c=_conv(a,a); M=float(c.max())
  if M<=1e-300: return a
  cs= -np.sort(-c) # sort desc
  idx=min(50,len(cs)-1); gap=cs[0]-cs[idx] if idx>0 else 0
  base=max(gap,M*0.02)
  factors=[1,0.5,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03,0.015,0.007,0.003,0.001,0.0004,0.00015,0.00005]
  # maybe if n large fewer
  _STATE['n']=n
  for factor in factors:
     if time.monotonic() > deadline - min_time_remaining: break
     rem = deadline - min_time_remaining - time.monotonic()
     if rem < 2: break
     beta=base*factor
     _STATE['beta']=beta
     est = 0.02*(n/2048.0)
     maxit = int(min(4000, max(40, rem/(est*15.0))))
     try:
       res = minimize(_state_fun, z, jac=_state_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', options={...})
       if res.fun finite and res.x finite:
          z_new=res.x; z_new=clip; z_new-=mean
          a_new=softmax(z_new)
          sc=_score(a_new)
          if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new.copy(); z=z_new
          else:
             # maybe still z from res? if not improve, keep previous z? L-BFGS could move but no score improve due smooth approximation; keep previous for stability.
             pass
     except Exception as e:
       print("lbfgs error",e); break
  return best_a
```
Maybe if a_new has score slightly worse but smoothed objective lower, future betas might benefit; but for hard score, keep best. Could also update z if within 0.01; not.

Pseudo exact:
```
def _sg_polish(a_init, deadline, tag=""):
    a=_normalize(a_init); best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a); z=_to_z(a)
    n=len(a); lr=0.1; patience=0; max_steps=int(100000)
    # variant list in order
    variants = ("soft1", "hard20", "soft2", "hard50", "soft3")
    for it in range(max_steps):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        var=variants[it % 5]
        a=softmax(z); c=conv(a,a); M=float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M): break
        if var == 'soft1': w=_soft_weights(c,M,M*1e-2)
        elif var == 'soft2': w=...1e-3
        elif var == 'soft3': w=...3e-4
        elif var == 'hard20': w=_top_weights(c,20)
        elif var == 'hard50': w=_top_weights(c,50)
        else: ...
        h=_conv(w, a[::-1])[n-1:2*n-1]
        W=float(np.dot(w,c))
        grad=a*(h-W)
        gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
        if gnorm < 1e-18:
             lr*=0.5; patience+=1; continue
        d=grad/gnorm
        cur_sc=best_sc  # use best score not necessarily score(a)? a might be from z (best), so equal
        best_z=z; best_sc_local=cur_sc
        steps=(lr, 0.5*lr, 0.25*lr, 0.125*lr, 2.0*lr)
        for st in steps:
            zn=z - st*d
            zn=np.clip(zn,-70,70); zn=zn-zn.mean()
            an=softmax(zn); sc=_score(an)
            if sc < best_sc_local - 1e-15:
               best_sc_local=sc; best_z=zn
        if best_sc_local < cur_sc - 1e-12:
            z=best_z; a=softmax(z); best_sc=best_sc_local; best=a.copy(); lr=min(lr*1.15, 1.0); patience=0
        else:
            lr*=0.65
            patience+=1
            if patience > 25:
               # restart from best with perturbation
               z=_to_z(best)
               z += 0.03*_RNG.standard_normal(n)
               z=clip; z-=mean
               lr=0.1; patience=0
    # final optional tiny candidate: z itself score
    a_end=softmax(z); sc_end=_score(a_end)
    if sc_end < best_sc: best=a_end; best_sc=sc_end
    return best, best_sc
```
Need `_soft_weights` and `_top_weights` top-level.

```

w=zeros; w[idx]=1/k
# But if some c idx negative? c nonneg. If M=0 fallback all zeros? return uniform? 
```

Let's check `_make_sqrt` recurrence formula for central binomial/4^i: c_i = c_{i-1}*(2i-1)/(4i). For i=1: (1)/(4)=0.25, but central binom C(2,1)/4=2/4=0.5. Formula wrong! Let's derive: c_i = binom(2i,i)/4^i. c_i/c_{i-1} = [4i(4i-1)/(2i(2i-1))]/4 = (2(4i-1))/(4(2i-1))? Compute: binom(2i,i)=binom(2i-2,i-1)*(2i)(2i-1)/(i^2). Dividing by 4 => ratio = (2i)(2i-1)/(4 i^2)= (2i-1)/(2i). Wait check i=1: (1)/(2)=0.5 correct. So ratio=(2*i-1)/(2*i), not 4i. Because 4^i already in denominator. Let's verify i=2: c2=6/16=0.375; ratio from c1 .5*(3/4)=0.375. Good. Use `(2.0*i - 1.0)/(2.0*i)`. This sequence decays like 1/√(πi). Sum as before. Good catch.

`_make_sqrt` loop O(n). For n=8192, a_i ~0.0125, sum~100? Wait 2√(n/π)=51 for 8192? sqrt(2610)=51. Good.

Power seed p: t**p. For p=0, returns ones. For p negative, t^p okay. For p=0.5, asympt f=sqrt{x}? The continuous p family a_i~i^{-0.5}? sqrt seed ~i^{-0.5}; power p=-0.5 similar but finite. Good.

Logistic: a=1/(1+exp(-s*(x-m))). If s=8,m=0.5, okay.

Random log-normal: `_make_random_log` uses `_RNG`; top-level. It does moving average. Use smooth min(32,n). If n small, smooth1.

`_normalize`: `a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(); if a.size==0: return np.ones(16); a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0); s=float(a.sum()); if not finite or s<1e-12: return np.ones(len(a if len else 16))/...; return a/s`. If a has NaN, clip makes NaN? `np.clip` with NaN returns NaN; sum NaN. Check `np.all(np.isfinite(a))` before? Use `a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)`. Good.
```
a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
```

`_add_candidate` skips if size<2.

`_interpolate` if a length 0? return uniform n. `a=_normalize(a)` ensures.

Now, final code.

Let's ensure no type hints? Not necessary but okay. Use simple.

One concern: The problem says "Make all helper functions top level and have no closures from function nesting. Don't use any lambda functions." The top-level helper `_state_fun` uses global `_STATE`, okay. Class exception top-level okay.

Now, final output only code fenced. Need no explanation.

Let's produce final code.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

This solver combines:
  - several constructive seeds (sqrt-binomial, power/logistic profiles, parent);
  - L-BFGS on log amplitudes using a log-sum-exp smoothing of the peak
    convolution;
  - hard-active-set subgradient polish with adaptive line search and
    diverse active-set choices;
  - cropping/cyclic reorder heuristics to exploit the explicit n factor.

All helper functions are top-level and lambda-free.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


class _StateTimeout(Exception):
    pass


_STATE = {"beta": 0.0, "n": 0, "deadline_stop": 0.0}
_RNG = np.random.default_rng(123456789)


def _next_pow2(n):
    k = 1
    while k < max(2, int(n)):
        k <<= 1
    return k


def _conv(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    out_n = a.size + b.size - 1
    if out_n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    N = _next_pow2(out_n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)[:out_n]
    r = np.nan_to_num(r, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(r, 0.0)


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64) / 16.0
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()) / (s * s))


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=70.0, neginf=-70.0)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -70.0, 70.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(z) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _to_z(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-30))
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=70.0, neginf=-70.0)
    return np.clip(z, -70.0, 70.0)


def _smoobj(z, beta, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e100
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _conv(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if (not np.isfinite(cmax)) or cmax <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0
    if beta <= 1e-300:
        return float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    sh = (c - cmax) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(sh, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 1e-300:
        return float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    return float(2.0 * n * (cmax + beta * np.log(s)))


def _smograd(z, beta, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _conv(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(z)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if (not np.isfinite(cmax)) or cmax <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros_like(z)

    if beta <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        sh = (c - cmax) / beta
        e = np.exp(np.clip(sh, -700.0, 0.0))
        s = float(e.sum())
        if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 1e-300:
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = e / s

    ar = a[::-1]
    wc = _conv(w, ar)
    h = wc[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    W = float(np.dot(w, c))
    g = 4.0 * float(n) * a * (h - W)
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return g


def _state_fun(z):
    if _STATE.get("deadline_stop", 0.0) and time.monotonic() > _STATE["deadline_stop"]:
        raise _StateTimeout
    if z is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return 1e100
    return _smoobj(z, _STATE["beta"], _STATE["n"])


def _state_jac(z):
    if _STATE.get("deadline_stop", 0.0) and time.monotonic() > _STATE["deadline_stop"]:
        raise _StateTimeout
    if z is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    return _smograd(z, _STATE["beta"], _STATE["n"])


def _soft_weights(c, M, beta):
    m = c.size
    if m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if beta <= 1e-300 or M <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    sh = (c - M) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(sh, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def _top_weights(c, k):
    m = c.size
    if m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(max(1, min(k, m)))
    if k >= m:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, m - k)[-k:]
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return w


def _threshold_weights(c, tol):
    m = c.size
    if m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    mask = c >= (M * (1.0 - float(tol)))
    cnt = int(mask.sum())
    if cnt <= 0:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[mask] = 1.0 / float(cnt)
    return w


def _run_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, tag=""):
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    if (not np.isfinite(best_sc)):
        best_sc = float("inf")
    z = _to_z(a)

    c = _conv(a, a)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if (not np.isfinite(M)) or M <= 1e-300:
        return a

    cs = -np.sort(-c)
    idx = min(50, max(1, cs.size - 1))
    gap = float(cs[0] - cs[idx]) if cs.size > idx else 0.0
    base_beta = max(gap, 0.05 * M)

    factors = [
        1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.007,
        0.003, 0.0012, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.00008, 0.00003
    ]

    _STATE["n"] = n
    stop = max(0.0, float(deadline) - float(min_time_remaining))
    _STATE["deadline_stop"] = stop
    no_improve = 0

    for factor in factors:
        if time.monotonic() > stop:
            break
        rem = stop - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 2.0:
            break

        beta = max(base_beta * factor, M * 1e-6)
        _STATE["beta"] = beta

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _state_fun,
                z,
                jac=_state_jac,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": 100000,
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxcor": 60,
                    "maxls": 30,
                },
            )
        except _StateTimeout:
            break
        except Exception:
            break

        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is None:
            break
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            continue

        x = np.clip(x, -70.0, 70.0)
        x = x - float(x.mean())
        a_new = _softmax(x)
        sc_new = _score(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new):
            if sc_new < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = float(sc_new)
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                z = x
                no_improve = 0
            elif sc_new < best_sc + 1e-8:
                z = x
                no_improve += 1
            else:
                no_improve += 1

    return best_a


def _sg_polish(a_init, deadline, tag=""):
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    if (not np.isfinite(best_sc)):
        best_sc = float("inf")
    z = _to_z(best_a)

    lr = 0.12
    patience = 0
    max_steps = 100000
    variants = ("soft_wide", "top20", "soft_mid", "thr2", "top80", "soft_sharp")

    for it in range(max_steps):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break

        var = variants[it % len(variants)]
        a = _softmax(z)
        c = _conv(a, a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        M = float(c.max())
        if (not np.isfinite(M)) or M <= 1e-300:
            break
        cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if var == "soft_wide":
            w = _soft_weights(c, M, 5e-3 * M)
        elif var == "soft_mid":
            w = _soft_weights(c, M, 1e-3 * M)
        elif var == "soft_sharp":
            w = _soft_weights(c, M, 2e-4 * M)
        elif var == "top20":
            w = _top_weights(c, 20)
        elif var == "top80":
            w = _top_weights(c, 80)
        else:
            w = _threshold_weights(c, 2.0e-3)

        ar = a[::-1]
        wc = _conv(w, ar)
        h = wc[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        if h.size != n:
            h = np.resize(h, n)
        W = float(np.dot(w, c))
        grad = a * (h - W)
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-20:
            lr *= 0.7
            patience += 1
            if patience > 30:
                z = _to_z(best_a)
                patience = 0
            continue

        d = grad / gnorm
        best_local_z = z
        best_local_sc = cur_sc

        for st in (lr, 0.5 * lr, 0.25 * lr, 0.125 * lr, 2.0 * lr):
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
                break
            zn = z - st * d
            zn = np.clip(zn, -70.0, 70.0)
            zn = zn - float(zn.mean())
            an = _softmax(zn)
            sc = _score(an)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_local_sc - 1e-15:
                best_local_sc = float(sc)
                best_local_z = zn

        if best_local_sc < cur_sc - 1e-13:
            z = best_local_z
            a = _softmax(z)
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_sc = best_local_sc
            lr = min(1.0, lr * 1.18)
            patience = 0
        else:
            lr *= 0.65
            patience += 1
            if patience > 35:
                z = _to_z(best_a)
                z = z + 0.035 * _RNG.standard_normal(n)
                z = np.clip(z, -70.0, 70.0)
                z = z - float(z.mean())
                lr = 0.12
                patience = 0

    a_end = _softmax(z)
    sc_end = _score(a_end)
    if np.isfinite(sc_end) and sc_end < best_sc:
        best_a = a_end.copy()
        best_sc = float(sc_end)
    return best_a, best_sc


def _refine(a_init, budget_s, tag=""):
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    sg_reserve = min(120.0, max(20.0, 0.10 * float(budget_s)))
    a = _run_lbfgs(a, deadline, sg_reserve, tag)
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        a, _ = _sg_polish(a, deadline, tag)
    return a, _score(a)


def _make_uniform(n):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def _make_power(n, p):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    t = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(t, float(p))
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    if a.sum() <= 0.0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    return _normalize(a)


def _make_sqrt(n):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * ((2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i))
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if a.sum() <= 0.0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    return _normalize(a)


def _make_logistic(n, slope, center):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = -float(slope) * (x - float(center))
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    a = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(z))
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    if a.sum() <= 0.0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    return _normalize(a)


def _make_random_smooth(n, amp=0.5, smooth=32):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    z = _RNG.standard_normal(n)
    k = int(max(1, min(int(smooth), n)))
    if k > 1:
        z = np.convolve(z, np.ones(k) / float(k), mode="same")
    z = float(amp) * z
    return _softmax(z)


def _interpolate(a, n):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return _normalize(b)


def _add_candidate(candidates, a, tag):
    if a is None:
        return
    b = _normalize(a)
    if b.size < 2:
        return
    sc = _score(b)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        candidates.append((float(sc), len(candidates), b, tag))


def _update_best(best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, seq, sc, label):
    if seq is None:
        return best_seq, best_sc, best_tag
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if seq.size < 1:
        return best_seq, best_sc, best_tag
    seq = np.nan_to_num(seq, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(seq.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 0.01:
        return best_seq, best_sc, best_tag
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = _score(seq)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        print(f"  NEW BEST: {sc:.10f} n={seq.size} [{label}]")
        return seq.copy(), float(sc), str(label)
    return best_seq, best_sc, best_tag


def _try_crops(best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, deadline):
    if best_seq is None:
        return best_seq, best_sc, best_tag
    n = best_seq.size
    fracs = (0.98, 0.95, 0.92, 0.88, 0.84, 0.80, 0.75, 0.70)
    tested = 0
    for fr in fracs:
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 2.0:
            break
        m = int(max(8, n * fr))
        m = min(m, n)
        a = best_seq
        cands = (
            a[:m],
            a[-m:] if m < n else a,
            a[(n - m) // 2:(n - m) // 2 + m] if m < n else a,
        )
        for c in cands:
            tested += 1
            if tested > 60:
                break
            b = _normalize(c)
            if b.size < 2:
                continue
            sc = _score(b)
            best_seq, best_sc, best_tag = _update_best(
                best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, b, sc, "crop"
            )
    return best_seq, best_sc, best_tag


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    global _RNG
    _RNG = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**63 - 1))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - 60.0)

    best_seq = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    best_tag = "none"

    parent = None
    n_parent = 0
    try:
        parent_raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        parent_raw = np.nan_to_num(parent_raw, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        parent_raw = np.clip(parent_raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(parent_raw.sum())
        if parent_raw.size >= 8 and s > 0.01:
            parent = _normalize(parent_raw)
            n_parent = parent.size
            if n_parent > 8192:
                parent = _interpolate(parent, 8192)
                n_parent = parent.size
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        n_parent = 0

    if parent is not None:
        sc = _score(parent)
        best_seq, best_sc, best_tag = _update_best(best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, parent, sc, "parent")

    candidates = []

    n_list = []
    for nn in (2048, 4096, 8192):
        if nn not in n_list:
            n_list.append(nn)
    if 1024 <= n_parent <= 8192 and n_parent not in n_list:
        n_list.append(n_parent)

    for ntry in n_list:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
            break

        if parent is not None:
            _add_candidate(candidates, _interpolate(parent, ntry), f"parent_resized_{ntry}")

        sqrt_a = _make_sqrt(ntry)
        rev_sqrt = sqrt_a[::-1].copy()
        _add_candidate(candidates, sqrt_a, f"sqrt_{ntry}")
        _add_candidate(candidates, rev_sqrt, f"rev_sqrt_{ntry}")

        x = np.arange(1, ntry + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        for p in (-0.25, -0.10, 0.0, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50):
            _add_candidate(candidates, _make_power(ntry, p), f"power_{p}_{ntry}")

        for cp in (-0.20, 0.0, 0.20):
            a2 = sqrt_a * np.power(x, cp)
            a2 = np.nan_to_num(a2, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            _add_candidate(candidates, a2, f"sqrt_power_{cp}_{ntry}")

        for sl in (2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
            for ctr in (0.35, 0.50, 0.65):
                _add_candidate(candidates, _make_logistic(ntry, sl, ctr),
                               f"log_{sl}_{ctr}_{ntry}")

        _add_candidate(candidates, _make_random_smooth(ntry, amp=0.45, smooth=32),
                       f"rand_smooth_{ntry}")

    candidates.sort()

    if candidates:
        for sc, _cnt, a, tag in candidates[:12]:
            if sc >= best_sc:
                continue
            best_seq, best_sc, best_tag = _update_best(best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, a, sc, tag)

    quick_end = min(t0 + 600.0, deadline - 240.0)
    n_quick_done = 0
    for sc, _cnt, a, tag in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= quick_end:
            break
        alloc = min(120.0, max(0.0, quick_end - time.monotonic() - 15.0))
        if alloc < 30.0:
            break
        a_opt, sc_opt = _refine(a, alloc, f"quick_{tag}")
        best_seq, best_sc, best_tag = _update_best(best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, a_opt, sc_opt, f"quick_{tag}")
        n_quick_done += 1
        if n_quick_done >= 5:
            break

    alt_seq = None
    for sc, _cnt, a, tag in candidates:
        if sc <= best_sc + 0.03 and not (best_seq is not None and np.array_equal(a, best_seq)):
            alt_seq = a
            break

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if best_seq is not None and rem > 80.0:
        if alt_seq is not None and rem > 200.0:
            long_budget = max(60.0, rem - 120.0 - 0.25 * rem)
        else:
            long_budget = max(60.0, rem - 30.0)
        a_opt, sc_opt = _refine(best_seq, long_budget, "long_best")
        best_seq, best_sc, best_tag = _update_best(best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, a_opt, sc_opt, "long_best")

    if alt_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        alt_budget = min(300.0, max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0))
        a_opt, sc_opt = _refine(alt_seq, alt_budget, "long_alt")
        best_seq, best_sc, best_tag = _update_best(best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, a_opt, sc_opt, "long_alt")

    best_seq, best_sc, best_tag = _try_crops(best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, deadline)

    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        n = best_seq.size
        for i in range(2):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15.0:
                break
            z = _to_z(best_seq)
            if i == 0:
                z = z + 0.04 * _RNG.standard_normal(n)
                label = "restart_noise"
            else:
                t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
                f1 = _RNG.integers(1, max(2, n // 8))
                f2 = _RNG.integers(1, max(2, n // 4))
                p1 = _RNG.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
                p2 = _RNG.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
                z = z + 0.02 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * t / n + p1)
                z = z + 0.02 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * t / n + p2)
                label = "restart_fourier"
            z = np.clip(z, -70.0, 70.0)
            z = z - float(z.mean())
            a_p = _softmax(z)
            alloc = min(60.0, max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0))
            a_opt, sc_opt = _refine(a_p, alloc, label)
            best_seq, best_sc, best_tag = _update_best(best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, a_opt, sc_opt, label)
            best_seq, best_sc, best_tag = _try_crops(best_seq, best_sc, best_tag, deadline)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = _make_uniform(2048)
        best_sc = _score(best_seq)
        best_tag = "fallback_uni"

    final = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s_final = float(final.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s_final)) or s_final < 0.01:
        final = _make_uniform(2048)

    try:
        if "evaluate_sequence" in globals() and time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
            eval_score = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final])
            if np.isfinite(eval_score):
                print(f"  evaluator check: {eval_score:.10f} n={final.size} tag={best_tag}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"  finalize: n={final.size} internal_score={_score(final):.10f} tag={best_tag} "
          f"elapsed={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s")
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
